Reasons from morphology, floras (FNA/Jepson/eFlora), voucher herbarium specimens (Darwin Core, Index Herbariorum), nomenclature (IPNI/POWO/Madrid Code), APG IV phylogeny, DNA barcoding (rbcL/matK/ITS2), community ecology (vegan adonis2/betadisper, TRY traits), and CITES/ABS ethics; treats vegetative mis-ID, pseudoreplication, PERMDISP confounds, and unvouchered GBIF records as first-class failure modes. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/tree/HEAD/scientific-agents/botanist/skills/botanist) · ★255 · 2 copies)
